Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the cellular framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 869. The resulting operator achieves bound O(936/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on cellular structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 869. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 936. QED.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 489 + O(1/n^248). Summing over all modes and applying the cellular structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
